Nation States
India
Brave AI
Q
  • Top Sexual Misconduct Cases In Indian Journalism Are
A
  • Prominent sexual misconduct cases in Indian journalism include the conviction of Tarun Tejpal, former editor of Tehelka, who was sentenced to 10 years in prison in August 2026 for raping a colleague in 2013. Another major case involved former External Affairs Minister M.J. Akbar, who resigned after dozens of women accused him of harassment; journalist Priya Ramani was acquitted in 2021 of defamation after accusing him.
  • Other high-profile allegations include those against KR Sreenivas, former Times of India editor, who resigned after multiple women accused him of sexual harassment, and Gautam Adhikari, who stepped down as senior fellow at the Center for American Progress after accusations of forcibly kissing journalists. The movement also exposed misconduct by CP Surendran, who resigned as director of a literary festival after eleven women accused him of harassment, and Mayank Jain, who resigned from Business Standard following accusations of being a "sexual predator."
  • The #MeToo movement in India, which gained significant momentum in 2018, saw 49 names emerge across journalism, film, and literature, highlighting systemic issues such as unwanted touching, inappropriate messages, and rape. These cases led to widespread resignations, internal inquiries, and public debates about workplace safety and the handling of harassment claims within the media industry.

Comedy Literature
Brave AI
Q
  • Best Comedy Literature on Sex
A
  • Humor and sexuality intersect in literature through romantic comedies, satirical takes on relationships, and bold explorations of intimacy. Based on the search results, here are standout books that blend comedy with sexual themes:
  • The Sexy Book of Sexy Sex by Kristen Schaal and Rich Blomquist
    • A satirical, illustrated guide that parodies traditional sex manuals with absurd advice, fictional testimonials, and outlandish diagrams. It’s both irreverent and insightful, offering laughs while commenting on societal norms around sex.
  • 44 Chapters About 4 Men by B.B. Easton
    • A candid, humorous memoir-in-disguise that chronicles the author’s tumultuous marriage and sexual awakening. Blending self-deprecation and raw honesty, it’s praised for its authenticity and comedic timing.
  • Wallbanger by Alice Clayton
    • A popular romantic comedy featuring sizzling chemistry and laugh-out-loud moments. The story follows a woman distracted by her sexy neighbor, exploring themes of attraction, commitment, and miscommunication with wit.
  • Yes Please by Amy Poehler
    • While not a novel, this comedic memoir weaves personal stories about relationships, sex, and fame with Poehler’s signature humor. It’s both empowering and hilarious, offering a feminist take on modern womanhood.
  • Uck It List by Jimmy Tudeski
    • Part of a series blending erotic content with slapstick humor and exaggerated characters. These books use absurd situations and wordplay to explore adult themes in a lighthearted, entertaining way.
  • These works exemplify how comedy can demystify and celebrate sex, using humor to navigate vulnerability, desire, and the quirks of human intimacy.
Q
  • Literature on Comedy and Sex
A
  • Some of the best literature that blends comedy with themes of sex and relationships combines sharp wit, absurdity, and candid observations about human intimacy.
  • Notable Books and Authors
    • Nicholson Baker is a standout author in this genre. His novel House of Holes is a surreal, playful, and wildly imaginative exploration of sexuality, framed as a hedonistic resort where fantasies come true. It's praised for its humor, creativity, and explicit yet consensual treatment of erotic themes.
    • Another classic is Giovanni Boccaccio’s The Decameron, a 14th-century collection of novellas told by people escaping the Black Death. Many of the tales are bawdy, clever, and sexually charged, using humor to critique social norms and human folly.
    • Helen Fielding’s Bridget Jones’s Diary offers a modern, humorous take on single life, love, and sexuality, told through a self-deprecating diary format that resonates with readers for its relatability and charm.
  • Contemporary and Quirky Picks
    • The Fermata by Nicholson Baker: A provocative and darkly comic novel about a man who can stop time and uses his power in ethically ambiguous, often sexual, ways.
    • My Dad Wrote a Porno (series): While originally a podcast, the book series gained fame for its hilariously bad erotic fiction, blending cringe and comedy.
    • 50 Shades of Alice in Wonderland: A satirical short story that parodies both classic literature and modern erotica with a light, comedic touch.
